if(k==='lineinf'&&has(f,'bayonetdrill'))a*=1.18; if(k==='lightinf'&&has(f,'rifling'))a*=1.27;if(k==='heavyart'&&has(f,'mortars'))a*=1.3;if(has(f,'sappers')&&attack&&s.provinces[where]?.fort)a*=1.08; if(u.type==='Пехота'){a*=globalMod(f,'infAttack');if(has(f,'professional_army')){a*=1.08;d*=1.09} a*=(1+(has(f,'drill')?.12:0)+(has(f,'flintlock')?.12:0)+(has(f,'rankfire')?.12:0)+(has(f,'platoonfire')?.12:0)+(has(f,'bayonets')?.11:0)); d*=(1+(has(f,'infantry_forms')?.12:0)+(has(f,'columns')?.1:0))} if(u.type==='Конница')a*=globalMod(f,'cavalry')*(1+(has(f,'cavalry')?.15:0)+(has(f,'cavalrycharge')?.14:0)); if(u.type==='Артиллерия')a*=globalMod(f,'artillery')*(1+(has(f,'artillery')?.2:0)+(has(f,'gribeauval')?.14:0)+(has(f,'powdermills')?.06:0)); base+=qty*a;def+=qty*d; } let terrain=loc.terrain==='mountain'?1.36:loc.terrain==='taiga'?1.21:loc.terrain==='hills'?1.16:1; let supply=landSupply(p,f,where);let morale=.6+(p.morale||70)/200; if(attack)return base*(has(f,'roads')?1.06:1)*(has(f,'generalstaff')?1.12:1)*(F[f].trait==='army'?1.1:1)*(s.stability[f]/170+.59)*(s.exhaustion[f]>45?.88:1)*supply; return def*(1+(s.provinces[where]?.fort||0)*.19)*(has(f,'fortress_school')?1.13:1)*terrain*(F[f].trait==='fort'?1.11:1)*globalMod(f,'fort')*(1+(p.depot||0)*.05)*morale*supply } function defendersVisible(i){return own(i)||s.spy[s.player][s.provinces[i].owner]>s.turn||N[i].adj.some(x=>own(x))} function militaryLoss(p,loss){let remaining=Math.max(0,Math.floor(loss));for(let k of ['militia','inf','lightinf','lineinf','fusiliers','cav','dragoons','hussars','art','lightart','grenadiers','cuirassiers','horseart','heavyart','guards']){let have=countUnit(p,k),n=Math.min(have,remaining);if(['inf','cav','art'].includes(k))p[k]-=n;else if(n&&p.units)p.units[k]-=n;remaining-=n;if(remaining<=0)break}} function battleText(label,from,to,attack,defend,lossA,lossD,won){return `

⚔ ${esc(label)}

${esc(N[from].name)} → ${esc(N[to].name)}

Атакующая сторона
Мощь${attack.toFixed(1)}
Потери${lossA}
Обороняющаяся сторона
Мощь${defend.toFixed(1)}
Потери${lossD}

${won?'Позиции противника захвачены.':'Наступление отбито, части отступили.'}

`} function march(from,to){let a=s.provinces[from],b=s.provinces[to],f=s.player; if(!own(from)||!N[from].adj.includes(to))return say('Марш возможен лишь в соседнюю сухопутную провинцию'); if(b.owner!==f&&!withWar(f,b.owner))return say('Для наступления необходимо объявить войну'); if(armies(a)<=1)return say('Для выхода нужен гарнизон хотя бы из двух полков'); let force=detachLand(a);if(armies(force)<1)return say('Нет войск для марша'); let duration=travelDays(from,to,force,f);let order={id:++s.orderSeq,kind:'army',f,from,to,force,remaining:duration,total:duration};s.orders.push(order); log(`➤ Приказ на марш: ${N[from].name} → ${N[to].name}, ${armies(force)} полк(ов), прибытие через ${duration} дн.`); drawAll() } function canSailFor(f,from,to){let a=N[from],b=N[to];if(!a.port||!b.port)return false;const za=seaZone(a),zb=seaZone(b),dist=distKm(a,b),range=has(f,'navigation')?2150:1300;return adjacentSea(za,zb)&&distx.from===from&&x.to===to))return say('Этот порт уже блокирован');s.blockades.push({from,to,owner:s.player});a.seaMove=1;log(`⚓ Морская блокада порта ${N[to].name}.`);drawAll()} function research(id){let t=TECH.find(x=>x.id===id),f=s.player;if(!t||has(f,id)||s.research[f])return; if(!techAccessible(f,t))return say(`Недостаточно технологий или образования (нужно ${t.eduMin||0})`); if(!spend(cost(t.cost)))return; let days=researchDays(t,f);s.research[f]={id,remaining:days,total:days}; log(`✦ Академия начинает исследование «${t.name}». Срок: ${days} дн.`); if($('modal').classList.contains('visible'))showTechTree();else drawAll() } function dip(op){const f=s.player,t=dipTarget;if(f===t)return;const base=has(f,'diplomacy')?25:35;if(op==='improve'){if(s.action<1||!spend(cost(base)))return;s.action--;s.relations[f][t]=clamp(s.relations[f][t]+25,-100,100);s.relations[t][f]=clamp(s.relations[t][f]+15,-100,100);log(`Отправлено державе «${F[t].name}».`)}else if(op==='peace'){if(!withWar(f,t))return say('Между странами нет войны');if(s.action<1)return say('Нет дипломатических приказов');let ours=regOf(f).length, мире.`)}else{s.wars=s.wars.filter(x=>x!==code(f,t));delete s.warStart[code(f,t)];s.exhaustion[f]=Math.max(0,s.exhaustion[f]-16);s.exhaustion[t]=Math.max(0,s.exhaustion[t]-16);log(`Подписан мирный +35');s.allies.push(code(f,t));log(`С «${F[t].name}» заключён союз.`)}s.action=Math.max(0,s.action-1)}else if(op==='trade'){if(withWar(f,t))return say('Невозможно торговать с maintenance(f){let salary=advisorsQuarter(f);let ps=regOf(f),rev=0,expense=salary,grain=0,wood=0,iron=0;let blockadeSet=new Set(s.blockades.filter(x=>x.owner!==f).map(x=>x.to));for(let {p,i} of infra=p.workshop*(has(f,'manufactory')?12:9)+p.market*(has(f,'banking')?9:7)+p.dock*2+p.road*(has(f,'roads')?2:1)+p.workshop*(has(f,'textile')?5:0)+p.market*(has(f,'mercantilism')?4:0);let winter=s.season===3;if(winter)grain*=.7;s.gold[f]+=Math.floor(rev-expense);s.food[f]+=Math.floor(grain);s.wood[f]+=Math.floor(wood);s.iron[f]+=Math.floor(iron);let bad=Math.abs(s.food[f]);s.food[f]=0;s.stability[f]=clamp(s.stability[f]-3,0,100);for(let {p} of ps)if(p.inf>1&&Math.random()<.14)p.inf--;if(f===s.player)log(`⚠ Голод и нехватка провианта. Финансовый кризис подрывает доверие к власти.')}if(s.tax[f]===0)s.stability[f]=clamp(s.stability[f]+1,0,100);if(s.tax[f]===2)s.stability[f]=clamp(s.stability[f]-1,0,100);if(s.exhaustion[f]>0)s.exhaustion[f]=clamp(s.exhaustion[f]-(!s.wars.some(x=>x.split(':').includes(String(f)))?4:1),0,100);if(f===s.player)s.finance={income:Math.floor(rev),expense:Math.floor(expense),grain:Math.floor(grain),wood:Math.floor(wood),iron:Math.floor(iron)}} function advanceResearch(){F.forEach((_,i)=>{let project=s.research[i];if(!project)return; if(--project.remaining<=0){if(!s.tech[i].includes(project.id))s.tech[i].push(project.id); let computerTurn(){for(let f=0;f150&&s.wood[f]>35){let r=ownProvs[Math.floor(Math.random()*ownProvs.length)];if(r.p.workshop<2&&Math.random()<.5){r.p.workshop++;s.gold[f]-=115;s.wood[f]-=20}else }}}}if(Math.random()<.035&&s.turn>7&&ownProvs.length>3&&s.relations[f][s.player]<-35&&!withWar(f,s.player)&&!withAlliance(f,s.player)&&ownProvs.some(({i})=>N[i].adj.some(j=>s.provinces[j].owner===s.player))){s.wars.push(code(f,s.player));s.warStart[code(f,s.player)]=s.turn;log(`⚠ aiPeace(){s.warStart=s.warStart||{};for(let key of [...s.wars]){let [a,b]=key.split(':').map(Number);let elapsed=s.turn-(s.warStart[key]??0);if(elapsed<7)continue;let events(){if(Math.random()>.17)return;let ps=regOf(s.player);if(!ps.length)return;let r=ps[Math.floor(Math.random()*ps.length)],event=Math.floor(Math.random()*5);if(event===0){s.gold[s.player]+=50;log(`Купцы города ${N[r.i].name} вернули таможенные долги. The old quarterly economy is retained for balance. const MONTHS=['января','февраля','марта','апреля','мая','июня','июля','августа','сентября','октября','ноября','декабря']; function dateLabel(){return `${s.day} ${MONTHS[s.month-1]} ${s.year}`} function researchDays(t,f){let scholar=(.70+edu(f)*.009)*globalMod(f,'research');let academy=has(f,'academies')?1.13:1;let techboost=(has(f,'universities')?1.07:1)*(has(f,'scientificmethod')?1.1:1)*(has(f,'technicalschools')?1.05:1);return Math.max(22,Math.ceil(t.time*55/(scholar*academy*techboost)))} function restoreSave(data){ if(!data||![5].includes(data.v)||data.provinces?.length!==N.length||!Number.isInteger(data.player)||data.player<0||data.player>=F.length)throw Error('Сохранение несовместимо с текущей картой'); pauseGame();mapSignature='';s=data;s.v=5;s.education=s.education||F.map(()=>25);s.advisers=s.advisers||F.map(()=>[]);s.month=s.month||s.season*3+1;s.day=s.day||1;s.orders=s.orders||[];s.production=s.production||[];s.orderSeq=s.orderSeq||0; for(let p of s.provinces)p.units=p.units||{}; for(let r of s.research){if(r&&r.total<=10){r.remaining*=55;r.total*=55}} selected=s.provinces[CAP[s.player]]?.owner===s.player?CAP[s.player]:regOf(s.player)[0]?.i??0; mission=null;drawAll();closeModal();drawClock(); } function pauseGame(){isPlaying=false;drawClock()} function togglePause(){if(!s||s.ended)return;if($('modal').classList.contains('visible'))return; isPlaying=!isPlaying;clockAccumulator=0;lastFrame=performance.now();drawClock()} function speedSet(i){gameSpeed=clamp(Number(i)||1,1,5);clockAccumulator=0;lastFrame=performance.now();drawClock()} function drawClock(){if(!s)return;$('date').textContent=dateLabel();$('clockstatus').textContent=isPlaying?'ВРЕМЯ ИДЁТ':'ПАУЗА';$('clockstatus').classList.toggle('running',isPlaying);$('playpause').textContent=isPlaying?'Ⅱ':'▶';$('playpause').title=isPlaying?'Пауза · Пробел':'Продолжить · Пробел';document.querySelectorAll('[data-speed]').forEach(b=>{b.classList.toggle('active',+b.dataset.speed===gameSpeed);b.setAttribute('aria-pressed',+b.dataset.speed===gameSpeed?'true':'false')})} const DAY_INTERVAL=[0,820,420,200,95,42]; function clockFrame(now){if(!lastFrame)lastFrame=now;let elapsed=Math.min(200,now-lastFrame);lastFrame=now; if(isPlaying&&!s.ended){clockAccumulator+=elapsed;let steps=0;while(clockAccumulator>=DAY_INTERVAL[gameSpeed]&&steps<12&&isPlaying){clockAccumulator-=DAY_INTERVAL[gameSpeed];advanceDay();steps++} if(steps){drawClock();if(steps>0&&(s.day%3===0||steps>=5||!isPlaying))drawAll();else refreshOrdersLayer()}} clockHandle=requestAnimationFrame(clockFrame)} function advanceDay(){if(!s||s.ended)return; let next=new Date(s.year,s.month-1,s.day+1,12);let oldMonth=s.month,oldQuarter=Math.floor((oldMonth-1)/3); s.year=next.getFullYear();s.month=next.getMonth()+1;s.day=next.getDate();s.season=Math.floor((s.month-1)/3); advanceResearch();advanceProduction();advanceOrders(); if(s.day===1&&oldMonth!==s.month){improveEducationMonthly();monthlyUpdate();if(s.season!==oldQuarter)quarterTurn()} if(s.ended)pauseGame() } function monthlyUpdate(){for(let f=0;fq.f===f&®IMENTS[q.key]).length; if(training>25)s.stability[f]=clamp(s.stability[f]-1,0,100); } } function quarterTurn(){F.forEach((_,f)=>maintenance(f)); s.blockades=s.blockades.filter(x=>s.provinces[x.from]?.owner===x.owner&&vessels(s.provinces[x.from])>0&&withWar(x.owner,s.provinces[x.to].owner)); computerTurn();aiPeace();events();s.turn++;s.action=3; const ps=regOf(s.player); if(!ps.length){s.ended=true;showModal('

Конец династии

Ваше государство утратило все территории.

')} else if(ps.length>=Math.ceil(N.length*.47)){s.ended=true;showModal('

Великая империя

Ваше государство контролирует почти половину стратегической карты.

')} else log(`Квартальный отчёт: ${dateLabel()}. Провинций — ${ps.length}, состояние казны — ${fmt(s.gold[s.player])}.`); drawAll();saveQuiet() } function advanceProduction(){let finished=[]; for(let q of s.production){q.remaining--;if(q.remaining<=0)finished.push(q)} if(!finished.length)return; for(let q of finished){let p=s.provinces[q.province];if(p?.owner!==q.f){if(q.f===s.player)log(`⚠ Заказ в ${N[q.province].name} потерян после захвата провинции.`);continue} if(REGIMENTS[q.key]&&!['inf','cav','art'].includes(q.key))p.units[q.key]=(p.units[q.key]||0)+1; else p[q.key]=(p[q.key]||0)+1; if(q.f===s.player){log(`✓ ${N[q.province].name}: «${REGIMENTS[q.key]?.label||UNIT[q.key]?.label}» готов(а) к службе.`);if(s.autoPauseProduction)pauseGame()}} s.production=s.production.filter(q=>!finished.includes(q)); } function detachLand(p,limit=Infinity){let force={inf:0,cav:0,art:0,units:{},fort:0,depot:0,morale:p.morale};let leave=1,remaining=limit; for(let k of UNITKEYS){let available=countUnit(p,k);if(k==='inf'&&available>0){available--;leave=0} else if(leave&&available>0){available--;leave=0} let qty=Math.min(available,remaining);if(qty<=0)continue; if(['inf','cav','art'].includes(k)){force[k]+=qty;p[k]-=qty} else{force.units[k]=qty;p.units[k]-=qty} remaining-=qty;if(remaining<=0)break} return force} function addLand(dst,force){for(let k of ['inf','cav','art'])dst[k]=(dst[k]||0)+(force[k]||0); dst.units=dst.units||{};for(let [k,q] of Object.entries(force.units||{}))dst.units[k]=(dst.units[k]||0)+q} function landSupply(p,f,i){let local=s.provinces[i],deficit=s.food[f]<80?.82:1; let road=local?.road||0,depot=local?.depot||0;let isolation=local?.owner===f||local?.owner===undefined?1:.9; return clamp((.85+road*.055+depot*.075)*(has(f,'quartermasters')?1.07:1)*deficit*isolation,.62,1.14)} function travelDays(from,to,force,f){let n=N[to],dx=N[from].x-n.x,dy=N[from].y-n.y; let rough={plain:1,steppe:1.02,hills:1.22,taiga:1.35,mountain:1.75}[n.terrain]||1; let slower=Object.entries(force.units||{}).some(([k,v])=>v>0&&k==='heavyart')||force.art>2?1.27:1; let cav=(force.cav>0||(force.units?.hussars||0)>0)&&armies(force)===force.cav+(force.units?.hussars||0)?0.77:1; let d=(13+Math.sqrt(dx*dx+dy*dy)*.19)*rough*slower*cav; if(has(f,'roads'))d*=.9;if(has(f,'surveys'))d*=.91;if(has(f,'pontoons'))d*=.92;if(has(f,'studs')&&force.cav>0)d*=.93;if(has(f,'generalstaff'))d*=.82;if(s.provinces[from].road)d*=.9; return clamp(Math.round(d),6,95) } function seaTravelDays(from,to,f){let a=N[from],b=N[to],dist=Math.hypot(a.x-b.x,a.y-b.y),days=15+dist*.065; if(has(f,'rigging'))days*=.88;if(has(f,'navigation'))days*=.88;if(has(f,'coppering'))days*=.80; return clamp(Math.round(days),9,88)} function advanceOrders(){let arrive=[];for(let o of s.orders){o.remaining--;if(o.remaining<=0)arrive.push(o)}if(!arrive.length)return; s.orders=s.orders.filter(o=>!arrive.includes(o)); for(let o of arrive){if(o.kind==='army')resolveLandOrder(o);else if(o.kind==='fleet')resolveFleetOrder(o);else if(o.kind==='landing')resolveLandingOrder(o)} } function resolveLandOrder(o){let from=s.provinces[o.from],b=s.provinces[o.to],f=o.f; if(!b)return;let attacker=o.force;let enemy=b.owner; if(b.owner===f){addLand(b,attacker);b.morale=Math.round((b.morale+attacker.morale)/2);if(f===s.player)log(`➤ Армия прибыла в ${N[o.to].name}: ${armies(attacker)} полк(ов).`);return} if(!withWar(f,enemy)){if(from.owner===f){addLand(from,attacker)}if(f===s.player)log(`Приказ отменён: война с ${F[enemy].name} завершена.`);return} let A=power(attacker,f,true,o.to)*(.88+Math.random()*.24),D=power(b,enemy,false,o.to)*(.88+Math.random()*.24); if(v23RiverCrossing(o.from,o.to)){A*=v23AssaultFactor(f);if(f===s.player||enemy===s.player)log('Речная переправа: атакующая армия несёт дополнительный штраф.');} let won=A>D,lA=Math.min(armies(attacker)-1,Math.max(0,Math.ceil(armies(attacker)*(won?.19:.42)*Math.min(1,D/Math.max(A,1))))),lD=Math.min(armies(b),Math.max(1,Math.ceil(armies(b)*(won?.66:.22)*Math.min(1,A/Math.max(D,1))))); if(has(f,'fieldmedicine'))lA=Math.floor(lA*.81); militaryLoss(attacker,lA);militaryLoss(b,lD); s.exhaustion[f]=clamp(s.exhaustion[f]+(won?1:4),0,100);s.exhaustion[enemy]=clamp(s.exhaustion[enemy]+(won?4:1),0,100); if(won){b.owner=f;s._maprevision=(s._maprevision||0)+1;let remnants={inf:b.inf,cav:b.cav,art:b.art,units:{...b.units}};b.inf=b.cav=b.art=0;b.units={};addLand(b,attacker);b.morale=clamp(attacker.morale-8,35,95);b.unrest=65;s.prestige[f]+=4; if(f===s.player||enemy===s.player)log(`⚔ ${F[f].name} заняла ${N[o.to].name}. Потери ${lA} / ${lD} полк.`); }else{if(from.owner===f)addLand(from,attacker);else{let retreat=N[o.to].adj.find(x=>s.provinces[x].owner===f);if(retreat!==undefined)addLand(s.provinces[retreat],attacker)} if(f===s.player||enemy===s.player)log(`⚔ Атака на ${N[o.to].name} отбита. Потери ${lA} / ${lD} полк.`)} if(f===s.player||enemy===s.player){pauseGame();showModal(battleText('Сухопутное сражение',o.from,o.to,A,D,lA,lD,won))} } function resolveFleetOrder(o){let b=s.provinces[o.to],a=s.provinces[o.from],ships=o.force,f=o.f;if(!b)return; if(b.owner===f){b.sloop+=ships.sloop;b.frigate+=ships.frigate;b.line+=ships.line;if(f===s.player)log(`⚓ Эскадра прибыла в ${N[o.to].name}.`);return} if(!withWar(f,b.owner)){if(a.owner===f){a.sloop+=ships.sloop;a.frigate+=ships.frigate;a.line+=ships.line}return} let A=navalPower(ships,f)*(.9+Math.random()*.22),D=navalPower(b,b.owner)*(.9+Math.random()*.22),won=A>=D; let lost=won?Math.floor(vessels(ships)*.22):Math.ceil(vessels(ships)*.48),their=won?Math.ceil(vessels(b)*.65):Math.ceil(vessels(b)*.2); takeSeaLoss(ships,lost);takeSeaLoss(b,their); if(a.owner===f){a.sloop+=ships.sloop;a.frigate+=ships.frigate;a.line+=ships.line} if(f===s.player||b.owner===s.player){log(`⚓ Морское сражение у ${N[o.to].name}: ${won?'успешный рейд':'поражение эскадры'}.`);pauseGame();showModal(battleText('Морское сражение',o.from,o.to,A,D,lost,their,won))} } function resolveLandingOrder(o){let b=s.provinces[o.to],from=s.provinces[o.from],f=o.f,force=o.force; if(b.owner===f){addLand(b,force);if(f===s.player)log(`⚓ Десант прибыл в ${N[o.to].name}.`);return} if(!withWar(f,b.owner)){if(from.owner===f)addLand(from,force);return} let enemy=b.owner,A=power(force,f,true,o.to)*.78*(has(f,'navigation')?1.2:1),D=power(b,enemy,false,o.to)*(1+b.fort*.11); militaryLoss(force,lA);militaryLoss(b,lD); if(won){b.owner=f;b.inf=b.cav=b.art=0;b.units={};addLand(b,force);b.unrest=65;b.morale=58}else if(from.owner===f)addLand(from,force); if(f===s.player||enemy===s.player){log(`⚓ Десант у ${N[o.to].name}: ${won?'высадка успешна':'высадка отбита'}.`);pauseGame();showModal(battleText('Морской десант',o.from,o.to,A,D,lA,lD,won))} } function refreshOrdersLayer(){let g=$('orderslayer');if(!g||!s)return; return `${Math.max(0,o.remaining)}д`}).join('') } function endTurn(){/* Compatibility for older scripts: advance one day, not a full season. */advanceDay();drawAll()} function saveQuiet(){try{localStorage.setItem('empire_of_sails_v5_save',JSON.stringify(s))}catch(e){}} function provinceFill(n,p){if(mode==='political')return F[p.owner].color;if(mode==='terrain')return TERR[n.terrain][1];if(mode==='economy'){let k=n.wealth+p.workshop*3+p.market*2;return k<5?'#69737a':k<9?'#8c987f':k<14?'#a5a069':'#d6bc79'}if(mode==='unrest')return p.unrest<20?'#648b75':p.unrest<45?'#af9e61':p.unrest<70?'#be7659':'#b34a4d';let k=p.depot*3+p.road*2+(n.port?1:0);return k<1?'#654e56':k<3?'#77745d':k<5?'#719885':'#55a9a5'} function drawMap(){const focus=selected;let html='';for(let i=0;i`}$('regions').innerHTML=html;let labels='',markers='';for(let i=0;i${esc(isActive?n.name:cap?F[p.owner].name:'')}`;if((p.owner===s.player||isActive)&&(armies(p)>2||vessels(p)>1)){markers+=`${armies(p)>0?armies(p):'⚓'}`}}$('towns').innerHTML=labels;$('forces').innerHTML=markers;$('coast').setAttribute('d',GEO.land);$('seanames').innerHTML=GEO.labels.map(p=>`${esc(p.name)}`).join('');refreshOrdersLayer();$('legend').innerHTML=(mode==='political'?F.map((f,i)=>`${esc(f.name)}${i===s.player?' ★':''}`):`${{terrain:'Окраска по типу местности',economy:'Насыщенность хозяйства и рынков',unrest:'Красный — сильное недовольство',supply:'Развитие дорог и армейских складов'}[mode]}`);document.querySelectorAll('.mode').forEach(el=>el.classList.toggle('chosen',el.dataset.mode===mode));updateTransform()} function tip(e,i){const n=N[i],p=s.provinces[i],t=$('tooltip'),v=$('mapviewport');t.style.display='block';let rect=v.getBoundingClientRect();t.style.left=clamp(e.clientX-rect.left+14,6,rect.width-235)+'px';t.style.top=clamp(e.clientY-rect.top+10,6,rect.height-100)+'px';let visible=defendersVisible(i);t.innerHTML=`${esc(n.name)}

`)} function v6battleOpen(){v6battleInit();let b=s.tactical;if(!b)return;let land=b.kind==='land',active=b.units.find(u=>u.id===b.selected&&u.hp>0),alive=b.units.filter(u=>u.hp>0),already=alive.filter(u=>u.side==='player'&&u.acted).length;let grid='';for(let y=0;y<7;y++)for(let x=0;x<11;x++){let u=alive.find(v=>v.x===x&&v.y===y),symbol=u?``:'';grid+=``} let h=`

Ваши части${alive.filter(z=>z.side==='player').length}

${b.log.slice(-4).map(v6name).join(' · ')}

if(dist>range){let dx=Math.sign(closest.x-e.x),dy=Math.sign(closest.y-e.y),nx=e.x+dx,ny=e.y+dy;if(nx>=0&&nx<11&&ny>=0&&ny<7&&!b.units.some(z=>z.hp>0&&z.x===nx&&z.y===ny)){e.x=nx;e.y=ny}continue}let damage=Math.max(1,Math.round((e.type==='ship'?(e.fire||5):e.type==='art'?4:3)*(.7+Math.random()*.5)));if(b.kind==='land')damage=Math.max(1,Math.round(damage/globalMod(s.player,'infantryDef')));closest.hp-=damage;b.log.push(`${e.label}: ответный огонь, урон −${damage}.`)}
